公司簡介
Corporate Profile

                                             綠城中國控股有限公司是中國領先的優質房產品開發及生
                                             活綜合服務供應商，以優秀的產品質量、獨特的建築美
                                             學、以人為本的服務保持行業引領地位。公司連續二十一
                                             年榮獲「中國房地產百強企業綜合實力Top10」1 ，所進駐重
                                             點城市客戶滿意度連續十五年保持領先，並獲得「2025中
                                             國房地產百強企業穩健性Top10」1 、「中國房地產交付力優
                                             秀企業」1等榮譽。

                                             Greentown China Holdings Limited is a leading quality
                                             property developer and an integrated living service provider
                                             in China. It maintains a leadership position in the industry by
                                             virtue of the quality of its properties, its unique architectural
                                             aesthetics, and its customer-centric services. The Company
                                             has been awarded the “Top10 among 100 Chinese Real
                                             Estate Enterprises by Comprehensive Strength” 1 for 21
                                             consecutive years and maintained a leading position in
                                             customer satisfaction in core cities in which it operates for 15
                                             consecutive years. It has also received honors such as “Top10
                                             among 100 Chinese Real Estate Enterprises by Stability
                                             in 2025”1 and “China Excellent Real Estate Enterprises by
                                             Delivery Strength”1.


                                             綠城中國控股有限公司於1995年1月在中國杭州成立，2006年7月在香港聯交所上
                                             市，並先後引入九龍倉、中交集團作為重要股東。於本報告日期，綠城中國的最
                                             大單一股東中交集團持有本公司已發行股本總額約28.88%的權益。

                                             Greentown China Holdings Limited was founded in Hangzhou, China in January
                                             1995 and listed on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in July 2006. Wharf and CCCG
                                             were introduced as substantial shareholders successively. As at the date of this
                     杭州玉瀾月華                  report, CCCG, Greentown China’s single largest shareholder, was interested in
                     Hangzhou Yulan Yuehua   approximately 28.88% of the total issued share capital of the Company.



                                             附註：                           Note:

                                             1.     中指院                    1.      China Index Academy


002   Greentown China Holdings Limited 綠城中國控股有限公司
公司簡介
Corporate Profile




歷經三十年的發展，公司秉持「品質為先」的發展     Throughout 30 years of development, the Company has adhered to the
戰略指引，佈局房地產上下游業務，形成重資       development strategy of “quality first” and developed the upstream and
產、輕資產和「綠城＋」三大板塊。重資產板塊聚     downstream of the real estate business to form three major segments, namely
焦房產開發業務，持續精進產品力，打造綠城「好     asset-heavy, asset-light and “Greentown+”. The asset-heavy segment focuses
房子」； 輕資產板塊強化代建業務，2020年7月   on the real estate development business, and continuously enhances product
正式分拆綠城管理控股有限公司並於聯交所主板      strength to build Greentown “Good Houses”. The asset-light segment strengthens
上市（股票代碼：09979），市佔率在行業中遙遙   the project management business. In July 2020, Greentown Management
領先；「綠城＋」板塊發展和培育不動產產業鏈業     Holdings Company Limited was officially spun off and listed separately on the
務，建立產業生態圈，協同賦能主業。          Main Board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 09979), with a market share
                           ranking far ahead in the industry. The “Greentown+” segment develops and
                           cultivates the industry chain business of real estate and establishes an industrial
                           ecosystem to collaborate and empower its main business.


作為中國精品物業營造專家，綠城中國一直堅持      Being a professional developer of premium properties in China, Greentown China
創新，不斷探索人居關係，在營造美麗建築的基      has always insisted on innovation and continued to explore the relationship
礎上，致力於為更多人打造美好生活。公司立足      between humans and their living space. Through creating aesthetically pleasing
於浙江，聚焦核心區域，精耕重點城市及優質地      architecture, Greentown China is committed to building a better life for more
塊。綠城中國憑藉優質的開發品質、高質的土地      people. Based in Zhejiang, the Company focuses its attention on core areas as
儲備、高效的組織架構、優秀的管理團隊，在業      well as cultivating footprints in key cities and quality land parcels. Leveraging its
務所及的城市成功樹立品牌形象，並累積了大量      superior development quality, premium land bank, highly effective organizational
高質量項目的開發經驗和卓越的營運能力，為可      structure and high-calibre management team, Greentown China has established
持續發展提供了強勁動力。               its brand image in all the cities where it operates. The rich experience that
                           Greentown has accumulated in developing numerous high-quality projects
                           together with its outstanding operational capabilities has served to provide strong
                           momentum for its sustainable development.


一直以來，公司堅守「真誠、善意、精緻、完美」     The Company has always adhered to its corporate values of “Sincerity, Kindness,
的企業價值觀，堅持做好企業、造好房子、為好      Exquisiteness, Perfection”, and has achieved encouraging progress on the path of
生活，並取得可喜的進步。2025年上半年，公司    “being an excellent enterprise, constructing good houses and creating wonderful
持續積蓄發展動能，提升經營質量，合同銷售額      lives”. In the first half of 2025, the Company has been continuously accumulating
約人民幣1,222億元，位列行業第二，新增貨值位   development momentum and improving operational quality, with contracted
列行業第三。                     sales of approximately RMB122.2 billion, ranking second in the industry, and
                           newly-added saleable value ranking third in the industry.


面向未來，公司將保持戰略定力，以打造「Top10   Looking ahead, the Company will adhere to its strategic principle with the aim
中的品質標桿」為目標，以「最懂客戶、最懂產      of building the “quality benchmark among the Top10” and the strategic pivot
品」為戰略支點，持續踐行「以客戶為中心的產品     points of “best understanding of customers and best understanding of products”,
主義」，同時以「高顏值、極賢惠、最聰明，房低     continue to implement its strategy of “customer-oriented product offering”, while
碳、全週期、人健康」為目標建設綠城「好房子」，    building Greentown “Good Houses” with the goal of being “gorgeous, extremely
不斷滿足人們對美好生活的追求。            virtuous, the smartest, low-carbon, full-cycle and healthy”, and constantly meet
                           people’s pursuit of better life.

010   Greentown China Holdings Limited 綠城中國控股有限公司
管理層討論與分析
Management Discussion and Analysis




經營回顧                                 Operation Review
2025年上半年，全球經濟形勢複雜多變，國內               In the first half of 2025, the Chinese economy faced significant challenges amid
經濟在挑戰中承壓前行，房地產市場延續低位盤                a complex and volatile global economic landscape, with the real estate market
整。中央和地方穩市場政策持續發力，地產行業                undergoing adjustment at a low level. The central and local government policies
朝著止跌回穩的方向邁進，重點城市的新房市場                that aimed at market stabilization took effect, guiding the property sector toward
出現企穩蹟象，部分城市在政策刺激下成交量有                a gradual recovery. Signs of stabilization emerged in the new housing market in
所回升，但整體仍處於「弱復蘇、強分化」的築底               key cities, and the transaction volumes in some cities rebounded under policy
階段。行業規模持續收縮，新建商品房銷售面積                stimulus. However, the overall market remained in the bottoming stage of “weak
及銷售額仍同比下降； 市場持續下行，房企開                recovery with strong divergence”. Particularly, the industry’s scale continued
發投資額降幅較去年末進一步擴大，房企到位資                to shrink, as evidenced by a year-on-year decrease in sales area and sales
金增速延續下跌，房地產開發景氣指數呈現回落                amount of new commercial properties. The decline in investment in real estate
趨勢；新房及二手房價格持續下跌，同時庫存攀                development widened further compared to the end of last year, while the growth
升，壓力持續。整體而言，行業復蘇的力度和持                of actual funds available for real estate companies continued to decline, and
續性仍面臨諸多不確定性。                         the industry’s prosperity index showed a downward trend, reflecting a market
                                     trend tilting to the downside. Prices of new and second-hand housing were going
                                     down, with ongoing pressure from mounting inventory. Overall, uncertainties
                                     remained over the strength and sustainability of the industry’s recovery.

識，將攻堅去化、增收節支、降本增效和品質交                for success”, and has remained alert, and maintained bottom-line thinking and
付視為底線任務，持續推動營銷創新、產品進                 consciousness of change since the beginning of the year. The Company has
階、運營迭代及組織變革，通過投資引領、代建                prioritized key tasks such as tackling inventory challenges, revenue enhancement
升維、業務突破和戰略升級謀求「全品質、高質                and expense saving, cost reduction and efficiency improvement as well as

產品端：持續迭代，優勢領跑                        Product: continuous iteration with leading advantages
產品品質是綠城中國的「一號工程」，上半年持續               Product quality is the “No.1 Project” of Greentown China. In the first half of
迭代綠城好房子，以產品力帶動經營效益的提                 the year, Greentown China continued to iterate its good houses and drove
升，有力支撐經營。                            the improvement of operational efficiency by product strength, which strongly
                                     supported its operations.


好房子實踐，引領行業。一方面加強「好房子」前               The practice of good houses leading the industry. On the one hand, Greentown
瞻技術引領，更新綠城「好房子」產品標準，並以               China strengthened the forward-looking technology leadership for “Good
經營價值為導向，以「好房子」實踐為基礎，向                Houses”, updated the product standards of Greentown “Good Houses”, and
各項目直接提供可實踐的「好房子」實操IP技術手              provided practical IP technology manuals of “Good Houses” to each project
冊；另一方面，大力推進「好房子」核心技術系統               directly based on “Good Houses” practices and guided by operating values.
的落地應用，同步開展隔聲降噪、同層排水、三                On the other hand, Greentown China vigorously promoted the application of
聯供空調系統等「好房子」技術深化工作，積極引               the core technology systems of “Good Houses”, and simultaneously launched
領行業實踐。                               the deepening of “Good Houses” technologies such as soundproof and noise

前景展望                                 Prospects
經歷本輪房地產行業的深度調整，政策導向已從                Following the deep adjustments in the real estate industry, policy focus has
「去摃桿」轉向「防風險」，市場需求從「普漲」轉向             shifted from “deleveraging” to “risk prevention”, market demand has transitioned
「分化」，行業競爭從「規模的擴張」轉向「質量的較             from “broad-based growth” to “differentiation”, and industry competition has
量」，但不變的是城鎮化進程的長期趨勢、人民對               evolved from “scale expansion” to “quality competition”. However, the long-
美好生活的嚮往以及中央「房住不炒」的總基調。               term trend of urbanization, the people’s aspiration for a better life, and the
展望2025年下半年，行業預期仍處於盤整期，市              central government’s overarching principle of “houses are for living in, not for
場全面回暖仍需時間。核心城市憑藉改善需求和                speculation” remain unchanged. Looking ahead to the second half of 2025, the
政策支持韌性較強，而三四線城市面臨長期去庫                industry is still expected to undergo adjustments, and it will take time for a full
存壓力。                                 market recovery. Core cities will remain resilient driven by improved housing
                                     demand and policy support, while third- and fourth-tier cities continue to face
                                     destocking pressure in the long run.

匯率波動風險                                          Risks of foreign exchange fluctuation
本集團之營運所在地主要在中華人民共和國，絕                           The principal place of operation of the Group is in the PRC, and the majority
大部分的收入與支出均以人民幣結算。由於本集                           of its income and expenditure is settled in RMB. As the Group had deposits in
團存有外幣存款、外幣應收及應付關聯人士款項                           foreign currencies, amounts due from and to related parties and third parties
和第三方款項以及尚有外幣銀行借款和境外優先                           denominated in foreign currencies, as well as bank borrowings in foreign
票據，故本集團面對外匯風險。本期本公司購買                           currencies and overseas senior notes, the Group was exposed to foreign
交叉貨幣利率掉期及外匯遠期合約共計8.4億美元                         exchange risks. During the Period, the Company purchased cross currency
以減少外幣債務的匯率風險，平均降低融資成本                           interest rate swaps and foreign exchange forward contracts with an aggregate
2.3個百分點。本期產生匯兌淨收益人民幣0.36億                       amount of US$840 million to mitigate the foreign exchange risk of its debts
元。（2024年同期：虧損人民幣0.74億元）。                        denominated in foreign currencies, lowering finance costs by an average
                                                of 2.3 percentage points. Net foreign exchange gain of RMB36 million (the
                                                corresponding period of 2024: loss of RMB74 million) was incurred during the

公司釐定的各自表現目標，即相關承授人在最近                  meeting his/her performance targets as determined by the Company (i.e.
一次的本公司考核中須被認定為「稱職」或以上。                 the relevant grantee needs to be graded as “competent” or above in the
本集團已為僱員建立標準的目標考核體系，以評                  latest assessment by the Company). The Group has established a standard
估其業績表現和對本集團的貢獻，考核內容主要                  performance appraisal system for its employees to evaluate their performance
包括相關承授人的經營業績達成情況，以及他╱                  and contribution to the Group, and the performance assessment primarily
她是否觸犯本公司管理紅線等。本公司將根據承                  involves assessing the relevant grantee’s business achievements and whether
授人相關年度的表現考核結果，確定承授人是否                  he/she has breached the “red-lines” of the Company’s management policies,
達到個人表現目標。                              among other things. The Company will determine whether the grantees meet the
                                       individual performance target based on their performance appraisal results for
                                       the relevant year.

購股權估值                                            Valuation of Options
本公司採用二項式估值模式及柏力克舒爾斯期權                            The Company has been using the Binomial Valuation Model and the Black-
定價模式（統稱「模式」）為所授購股權估值。模式                          Scholes Pricing Model (collectively, the “Models”) to value the share options
所用的主要參數及在2017年、2018年、2020年及                      granted. Details of the key parameters used in the Models and the corresponding
2023年所授出購股權的相關公平值載於簡明綜合                          fair values of the options granted in 2017, 2018, 2020 and 2023 are set out in note
財務報表附註26。                                        26 to the condensed Consolidated Financial Statement.


股份激勵計劃                                           Share Award Scheme
本公司已採納股份激勵計劃（「股份激勵計劃」），                          The Company has adopted a share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”),
根據該計劃，本公司可安排受託人（「受託人」）從                          pursuant to which, the Company can arrange the trustee (the “Trustee”) to
公開市場為該計劃設立的信託（「信託」）購買待獎                          purchase the Shares to be awarded for the trust constituted for the Scheme (the
勵的股份，並為經選定僱員以信託形式持有，                             “Trust”) from the open market and held on trust for the selected employees until

第2410號由實體的獨立核數師審閱中期財務資料                         Engagements 2410 Review of Interim Financial Information Performed by the
進行審閱。中期財務資料的審閱包括主要向負責                           Independent Auditor of the Entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified
財務及會計事務的人員作出查詢，並應用分析性                           Public Accountants. A review of interim financial information consists of making
及其他審閱程序。審閱範圍遠小於根據香港核數                           inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting matters,
準則進行審核的範圍，故不能令我們保證我們將                           and applying analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially
知悉在審核中可能發現的所有重大事項。因此，                           less in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards
我們不會發表審核意見。                                     on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we
                                                would become aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit.
                                                Accordingly, we do not express an audit opinion.

附註：                                                                                            Notes:

(i)           特別儲備主要是指部分收購或出售附屬公司所致本公                                                          (i)          The special reserve mainly represents changes in equity attributable to owners of the Company
              司股東應佔權益的變動。該變動為根據非控股股東權                                                                       arising from partial acquisition or disposal of subsidiaries. The changes are calculated based on
              益經調整金額與支付或收取部分收購或出售代價的公                                                                       the difference between the amount by which non-controlling interests are adjusted and the fair
              平值之間的差額計算得出。                                                                                  value of the consideration paid or received for the partial acquisition or disposal.

(ii)          法定儲備不可分派，該儲備的轉撥金額由本集團有關                                                          (ii)         The statutory reserve is non-distributable and the transfer amount to this reserve is determined
              公司的董事會根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）有關法律                                                                     by the boards of directors of the relevant companies in the Group in accordance with the
              法規釐定。該儲備於取得有關機關批准後，可用於抵                                                                       relevant laws and regulations of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). This reserve can
              銷累計虧損及增加資本。                                                                                   be used to offset accumulated losses and increase capital upon approval from the relevant
                                                                                                            authorities.

(iii)         於本期附屬公司非控股股東注資人民幣1,670,781,000                                                   (iii)        Capital contributions from non-controlling shareholders of subsidiaries, amounting to
              元乃轉撥自應付非控股股東款項。                                                                               RMB1,670,781,000, were transferred from amounts due to non-controlling shareholders during

1.    公司資料                                      1.       CORPORATE INFORMATION
      綠城中國控股有限公司（「本公司」）於2005                             Greentown China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated in
      年8月31日根據公司法（2004年修訂本）於                             the Cayman Islands on 31 August 2005 as an exempted company with
      開曼群島註冊成立為一家獲豁免有限公                                  limited liability under the Companies Law (2004 Revision) and its shares
      司，其股份於2006年7月13日於香港聯合                              have been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
      交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。                                Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) with effect from 13 July 2006.


      本公司乃投資控股公司。其附屬公司（連同                                The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activity of
      本公司統稱為「本集團」）的主要業務為於中                               its subsidiaries (together with the Company referred to as the “Group”) is
      國發展供銷售住宅物業。                                        the development for sale of residential properties in the PRC.

      綜合財務資料是按國際會計準則（「國際會                                months ended 30 June 2025 has been prepared in accordance with

      簡明綜合財務資料並不包括年度財務報表                                 The interim condensed consolidated financial information does not
      中規定的所有資料及披露事項，且應與本                                 include all the information and disclosures required in the annual financial
      集團截至2024年12月31日止年度的年度綜                             statements, and should be read in conjunction with the Group’s annual
      合財務報表一併閱讀。                                         consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2024.

3.    會計政策變動及披露（續）                             3.    CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND DISCLOSURES
                                                     (continued)
      經修訂國際財務報告準則會計準則的性質                             The nature and impact of the amended IFRS Accounting Standard are
      及影響如下所述：                                       described below:


      國際會計準則第21號的修訂訂明實體應如                            Amendments to IAS 21 specify how an entity shall assess whether a
      何評估某一貨幣可否兌換為另一種貨幣及                             currency is exchangeable into another currency and how it shall estimate
      於缺乏可兌換性時應如何估計於計量日期                             a spot exchange rate at a measurement date when exchangeability is
      的即期匯率。該等修訂規定披露有助財務                             lacking. The amendments require disclosures of information that enable
      報表使用者了解不可兌換貨幣的影響的資                             users of financial statements to understand the impact of a currency not
      料。由於本集團進行交易的貨幣與集團實                             being exchangeable. As the currencies that the Group had transacted
      體用於轉換為本集團呈報貨幣的功能貨幣                             with and the functional currencies of group entities for translation into
      屬可兌換，因此該等修訂並無對中期簡明                             the Group’s presentation currency were exchangeable, the amendments
      綜合財務資料產生任何影響。                                  did not have any impact on the interim condensed consolidated financial
                                                     information.

10.   稅項（續）                                      10.      TAXATION (continued)
      本公司於香港註冊成立的附屬公司並無作                                  No provision for income tax has been made for the Company’s
      出所得稅撥備，乃由於其本期於香港並無                                  subsidiaries incorporated in Hong Kong as they had no assessable profits
      獲得任何應課稅利潤。                                          derived from Hong Kong during the period.


      根據中華人民共和國企業所得稅法（「企業                                 Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Enterprise Income Tax
      所得稅法」）及企業所得稅法實施條例，中                                 (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT Law, the tax rate
      國附屬公司的稅率為25%，但若干中國附                                 of the PRC subsidiaries is 25%, except for certain PRC subsidiaries which
      屬公司享有15%優惠稅率。                                       were taxed at a preferential rate of 15%.


      企業所得稅法豁免兩家有直接投資關係的                                  The EIT Law provides that qualified dividend income between two resident
      居民企業的合資格股息收入繳納所得稅。                                  enterprises that have a direct investment relationship is exempted from
      除此之外，該等股息須按企業所得稅法繳                                  income tax. Otherwise, such dividends will be subject to a 5% or 10%
      付5%或10%預扣稅。5%或10%的預扣稅率                              withholding tax under the EIT Law. A 5% or 10% withholding tax rate is
      適用於本集團。期內，根據本集團的營運                                  applicable to the Group. During the period, the directors of the Company,
      及發展計劃，本公司董事估計中國附屬公                                  based on the Group’s operation and expansion plan, estimated that part
      司保留盈利的部分將留存在中國內地，以                                  of the retained earnings of subsidiaries in the PRC would be retained
      作日後營運及投資之用。董事認為，該等                                  in Mainland China for use in future operations and investments. In the

      級                                                   FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      管理層已評估，銀行結餘及現金、抵押銀                                  Management has assessed that the fair values of bank balances and
      行存款、貿易及其他應收款項、訂金及預                                  cash, pledged bank deposits, trade and other receivables, deposits and
      付款項、應收╱應付關聯人士款項、貿易                                  prepayments, amounts due from/to related parties, trade and other
      及其他應付款項以及銀行及其他借款流動                                  payables, the current portion of bank and other borrowings approximate
      部分的公平值與其賬面值相若，主要由於                                  to their carrying amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of these
      該等工具於短期內到期。                                         instruments.


      管理層負責釐定金融工具公平值計量的政                                  Management is responsible for determining the policies and procedures
      策及程序。於各報告日期，管理層分析金                                  for the fair value measurement of financial instruments. At each reporting
      融工具價值的變動情況，並釐定估值中所                                  date, management analyses the movements in the values of financial
      用的主要輸入數據。估值過程及結果經董                                  instruments and determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The
      事會一年討論兩次，以進行中期及年度財                                  valuation process and results are discussed with the board of directors
      務匯報。                                                twice a year for interim and annual financial reporting.


      金融資產及負債的公平值計入自願雙方可                                  The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included at
      於當前交易（脅迫或清盤銷售除外）中交換                                 the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a current
      該工具所需的金額。估計公平值使用以下                                  transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced or liquidation
      方法及假設：                                              sale. The following methods and assumptions were used to estimate the
                                                          fair values:


      按公平值計入損益的金融資產（指投資公司                                 The fair values of the financial assets at fair value through profit or loss,
      及銀行發行的理財產品）公平值按使用擁有                                 which represent wealth management products issued by investment
      類似條款、信貸風險及餘下到期期限的工                                  companies and banks, have been calculated by discounting the expected
      具目前可得的利率折讓預期未來現金流量                                  future cash flows using rates currently available for instruments with
      計算。於報告期末，本集團按公平值計入                                  similar terms, credit risk and remaining maturities. The Group’s own non-
      損益的金融資產的自身不履約風險獲評估                                  performance risk for financial assets at fair value through profit or loss as
      為不重大。                                               at the end of the reporting period was assessed to be insignificant.

      度有效對沖工具的貨幣掉期合同，以管理                             currency swap contract designated as highly effective hedging instrument

      公平值根據可觀察利率及匯率釐定。                               interest rate basis. The fair value of these hedging instruments was
